  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雪是一种复杂的几何材料，其宏观响应受密度、温度和微观结构拓扑演化控制，力学行为跨越弹性、塑性、粘性和破坏等多种机制。现有的数值方法难以有效模拟雪的大变形、演化自由表面和复杂边界相互作用，因此需要一种稳健的数值框架来准确描述雪的力学行为，并服务于车辆-雪相互作用等工程应用。

Method: 采用基于广义有限差分法（GFDM）的纯无网格强形式配点框架；核心贡献是将修正剑桥黏土（Modified Cam-Clay）本构模型整合其中，实现全局隐式混合（压力-速度）格式与本构返回映射算法的数值耦合。其中静水压力部分通过泊松方程求解并经过修正剑桥黏土返回映射校正，偏应力响应则通过数值粘性公式半隐式处理，实现了体积与偏应力贡献的分区处理。

Result: 数值结果显示该耦合框架具有算法稳定性，能有效施加边界条件，支持局部空间细化，并可准确评估复杂边界几何上的作用力。通过刚体耦合，框架还展示了在车辆-雪相互作用仿真中的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的公式为未来动态雪载荷对车辆结构作用的仿真提供了稳健的基础，表明该无网格框架在雪力学数值模拟领域具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Snow is a complex geomaterial whose macroscopic response is governed by density, temperature, and the topology of its evolving microstructure. Its mechanical behavior spans elastic, plastic, viscous, and failure dominated regimes, imposing significant challenges for numerical methods, which intends to simulate large deformations, evolving free surfaces, and complex boundary interactions. This work presents the first integration of a Modified Cam-Clay constitutive formulation for snow into a purely meshfree strong-form collocation framework based on the Generalized Finite Difference Method. The main methodological contribution is a numerical coupling that combines a global implicit mixed formulation for pressure and velocity with a constitutive return-mapping algorithm. The hydrostatic pressure contribution is obtained from a Poisson equation and subsequently corrected through the Modified Cam-Clay return-mapping procedure, while the deviatoric response is treated semi-implicitly using a numerical viscosity formulation. This partitioned treatment of the volumetric and deviatoric stress contributions enables stable simulations with comparatively large time steps while producing smooth spatial pressure fields. As a result, forces on complex boundary geometries can be evaluated accurately. Numerical results of this coupling illustrate the algorithmic stability of the framework, the effective imposition of boundary conditions, and the suitability of local spatial refinement. The feasibility of applying the framework to vehicle-snow interaction through rigid-body coupling is also illustrated. The presented formulation provides a robust basis for future simulations of dynamic snow loading on vehicle structures.

</details>


### [118] [Recovery of Integer Signals from Limited DFT Samples: Lattice Methods and Stability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17024)
*Howard Levinson,Isaac Viviano*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于格基的算法，用于从部分离散傅里叶变换（DFT）测量中恢复整数值信号。作者推导了保证成功恢复的参数估计，并量化了这些估计如何依赖于信号长度、初始猜测误差和采样DFT系数的数量，同时通过数值实验验证了理论预测与观测恢复阈值的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于从部分DFT测量中恢复整数值信号的问题，虽然已有基于格基的算法被提出，但缺乏对其成功恢复条件的参数化理论分析。理解这些算法在何种参数条件下能够稳定地恢复信号，是评估其实用性和指导参数选择的关键。

Method: 本文将信号恢复问题形式化为在适当构造的格中寻找短向量的优化问题，通过分析格参数（如格的距离、最短向量长度等）与测量精度之间的关系，推导出保证成功恢复的参数估计。理论分析刻画了恢复算法的稳定性，其中格参数与所需测量精度密切相关。

Result: 推导出了保证成功恢复的参数估计，明确了信号长度、初始猜测误差和采样DFT系数数量对恢复性能的影响。数值实验表明，理论预测与观测到的恢复阈值在大范围问题参数下高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于格基的部分DFT整数信号恢复算法提供了系统的理论稳定性分析，揭示了关键参数间的依赖关系，并通过实验验证了理论结果的准确性，为该类算法在实际应用中的参数设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We analyze lattice-based algorithms for recovering integer-valued signals from partial discrete Fourier transform (DFT) measurements. These algorithms formulate signal recovery as the problem of finding short vectors in an appropriately constructed lattice. We derive parameter estimates that guarantee successful recovery and quantify how these estimates depend on the signal length, the error of an initial guess, and the number of sampled DFT coefficients. The analysis characterizes the stability of the inversion algorithms, as the lattice parameters are closely related to the required measurement precision. Numerical experiments demonstrate close agreement between the theoretical predictions and observed recovery thresholds over a broad range of problem parameters.

</details>


### [119] [Improved Arrow-Hurwicz method for Stationary Inductionless Magnetohydrodynamics System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17035)
*Duygu Uludag,Fatma G. Eroglu,Aziz Takhirov,Songul Kaya*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的Arrow-Hurwicz迭代格式用于求解稳态无感磁流体动力学（MHD）系统。该方案的核心创新是在电流密度方程中引入新的惩罚项，以有效控制此类公式中常见的不利混合项，并证明该方法具有几何收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳态无感磁流体动力学系统的数值求解中，电流密度方程中常出现不利的混合项，这些项会影响迭代格式的收敛性和稳定性，需要设计有效的迭代方案来克服这一问题。

Method: 提出一种改进的Arrow-Hurwicz迭代格式，在电流密度方程中引入新的惩罚项，通过惩罚项来抑制和控制在离散化过程中产生的不利混合项，从而实现几何收敛。

Result: 理论上证明了该格式具有几何收敛性；数值实验表明该方案在不损失精度的前提下有效提高了求解效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的带惩罚项的Arrow-Hurwicz迭代格式是求解稳态无感MHD系统的有效方法，在保证精度的同时实现了高效且几何快速的收敛。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a new Arrow-Hurwicz iterative scheme designed to solve the steady inductionless magnetohydrodynamics system. The main feature of the proposed scheme is the introduction of new penalty terms in the current density equation. These terms play a central role in effectively controlling the unfavorable mixed terms that commonly arise in such formulations. The proposed method is shown to achieve geometric convergence. Numerical tests affirm the efficiency of the new scheme without compromising accuracy.

</details>


### [120] [Wavelet-based multilevel framework for $\ell_1$-regularized image deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17123)
*Danyh Tolah,Malena I. Español,Misha E. Kilmer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于小波的多层V-cycle框架，将IRLS、Split Bregman和Majorization-Minimization三种迭代求解器嵌入其中以求解大规模ℓ1正则化图像去模糊问题，通过离散小波变换定义层间传递算子，并利用广义交叉验证自动选择正则化参数。实验表明IRLS的加速超过一个数量级，且小波类型与信息传递策略之间存在交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模ℓ1正则化图像去模糊问题在保持锐利边缘的同时高效求解仍具显著计算挑战，现有方法难以兼顾求解效率与边缘保持，需要一种能够加速迭代求解的多层框架。

Method: 提出小波基多层框架，将IRLS、SB和MM三种迭代求解器嵌入多层V-cycle结构中。采用离散小波变换定义层间传递算子（粗化与延拓），正则化参数通过广义交叉验证（GCV）自动选择。设计了两种信息传递策略：一种仅将粗层解传递到细层，另一种额外传递求解器特有的辅助量，并系统比较其性能。

Result: 数值实验表明IRLS的计算节省最为显著，加速比超过一个数量级；MM和SB的计算差异较小。实验还发现：采用Haar小波时，传递辅助迭代量效果最佳；而采用Daubechies小波时，仅传递解效果最佳。

Conclusion: 小波多层级框架能有效加速ℓ1正则化图像去模糊问题的求解，特别是对IRLS方法加速效果显著；最佳性能取决于小波类型与信息传递策略的匹配，Haar小波配合辅助量传递、Daubechies小波配合解传递效果最优。

Abstract: Solving large-scale $\ell_1$-regularized image deblurring problems efficiently while preserving sharp edges remains a significant computational challenge. We propose a wavelet-based multilevel framework that embeds three iterative solvers, Iteratively Reweighted Least Squares (IRLS), Split Bregman (SB), and Majorization-Minimization (MM), within a multilevel V-cycle. Discrete wavelet transforms define the interlevel transfer operators, and regularization parameters are selected automatically by Generalized Cross Validation. Two information transfer strategies are introduced and compared: one transfers only the coarse solution to the fine level, while the other transfers solver-specific auxiliary quantities. Numerical experiments demonstrate substantial computational savings for IRLS, with speedups exceeding an order of magnitude, while MM and SB exhibit more modest computational differences. The experiments generally show that transferring auxiliary iterates performs best with Haar wavelets, whereas transferring only the solution performs best with Daubechies wavelets.

</details>


### [121] [Robust Projector-Splitting Runge-Kutta Integrators of Orders Two and Three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17157)
*Shiheng Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文构造了二阶和三阶的鲁棒投影分裂Runge-Kutta方法，用于动态低秩逼近。这些方法通过共同基阶段构造，在存在小奇异值时仍能保持精度，其局部和全局误差界与小奇异值无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态低秩逼近需要时间积分器在小奇异值存在时仍保持精度。标准积分器在面对小奇异值时可能丧失精度或稳定性，因此需要构造对该问题鲁棒的数值方法。

Method: 构造投影分裂Runge-Kutta方法，核心特征是"共同基阶段构造"：每个内部阶段和端点都通过将Lubich和Oseledets的实际投影分裂算法应用于Runge-Kutta增量来获得，且始终从时间步开始处的因子和行空间出发。在一致有界、Lipschitz、光滑性及法向分量假设下进行分析。

Result: 对于中点法（二阶）和三阶方法，当投影分裂因子化秩为r时，局部误差分别为C(h^3+hε_r)和C(h^4+hε_r)，全局误差分别为C(δ+ε_r+h^2)和C(δ+ε_r+h^3)。常数与小奇异值无关。每个阶段和输出保持秩r，原始基宽在计算中得以保留。

Conclusion: 所构造的投影分裂Runge-Kutta方法在存在小奇异值时仍保持设计阶数的精度，误差界中的常数不依赖于小奇异值，且所有阶段保持固定的秩r，为动态低秩逼近提供了稳健的高阶时间积分器。

Abstract: Dynamical low-rank approximation requires time integrators that remain accurate in the presence of small singular values. We construct robust projector-splitting Runge--Kutta methods of orders two and three. Their central feature is a common-base stage construction: every internal stage and the endpoint are obtained by applying the practical projector-splitting algorithm of Lubich and Oseledets to a Runge--Kutta increment, always from the factors and row space at the beginning of the time step. Under uniform boundedness, Lipschitz, smoothness, and normal-component assumptions, every computation in which the projector-splitting factorizations have rank $r$ has local errors $C(h^3+h\varepsilon_r)$ and $C(h^4+h\varepsilon_r)$, and global errors $C(δ+\varepsilon_r+h^2)$ and $C(δ+\varepsilon_r+h^3)$, for the midpoint and third-order methods, respectively. Here $\varepsilon_r$ bounds the normal component of the vector field and $δ$ is the initial error. The constants are independent of small singular values. Every stage and output has rank $r$, with the original basis width retained throughout the calculation.

</details>


### [122] [Improved Convergence of Multilevel Moving Least-Squares Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17441)
*Robert Durst,Holger Wendland*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了移动最小二乘逼近与多层格式相结合的方法，证明了在规则网格数据点上，这种组合确实能带来改进的收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动最小二乘逼近是一种流行的多元函数逼近方法，但高阶多项式的使用会带来更高的计算成本和数值不稳定性。此前研究显示低阶移动最小二乘与多层格式的组合具有优越的数值表现，本文旨在从理论上严格证明这一组合的改进收敛性质。

Method: 在规则网格数据点的假设下，对移动最小二乘与多层格式的组合进行理论分析，证明其收敛性改善结果。

Result: 证明了在数据点形成规则网格的条件下，移动最小二乘与多层格式的组合确实导致改进的收敛结果。

Conclusion: 移动最小二乘结合多层格式不仅是数值上优越的实践方案，而且在理论上（至少对于规则网格数据）被严格证明具有改进的收敛性质。

Abstract: Moving least-squares approximation is a popular method for approximating multivariate functions from given discrete data. For higher accuracy higher degree polynomials have to be used, resulting also in higher computational cost and numerical instabilities. Recently, the combination of low-order moving least squares with a multilevel scheme showed superior numerical behavior. In this paper we will prove, amongst other things, that such a combination of moving least-squares with a multilevel scheme indeed leads to improved convergence results, at least if the data sites form a regular grid.

</details>


### [123] [Regularization of Statistical Inverse Problems on Non-Reflexive Banach Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17533)
*Darrel K Joseph,M P Rajan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了非自反Banach空间上的统计逆问题，利用Tikhonov正则化结合任意凸泛函对未知参数进行稳定逼近，在Bregman距离下给出了误差的概率上界，并辅以数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆问题在机器学习与人工智能等领域有广泛的应用需求，然而现有研究多局限于自反Banach空间或Hilbert空间，对非自反Banach空间中带噪声观测的逆问题稳定逼近缺乏系统的理论分析。作者旨在将Tikhonov正则化推广到更一般的非自反Banach空间设定下，建立统计学习框架下的收敛性理论。

Method: 将定义域设为非自反Banach空间，值域设为度量空间上的实值函数空间。假设观测数据g由有限个独立同分布且服从未知概率测度ρ的数据点刻画。采用任意凸泛函作为正则化项的Tikhonov正则化方法获得正则化解，并在Bregman距离下进行收敛性分析，推导误差的概率上界。

Result: 在Bregman距离度量下导出了正则化解与真解之间的误差上界（以概率形式给出），并通过数值实验验证了理论结果的正确性和可行性。

Conclusion: 本文表明在非自反Banach空间框架下，采用任意凸泛函的Tikhonov正则化方法仍能实现对逆问题稳定逼近，且其统计学收敛保证可量化为概率意义上的误差上界，为该类问题提供了理论基础与实验支持。

Abstract: Inverse learning within a statistical framework has a wide range of applications. It has garnered significant attention in machine learning, artificial intelligence, and related fields, where the goal is to infer unknown parameters from indirect and noisy observations. This work investigates the stable approximation of $u^{\dagger}$ which solves the equation $Au=g$, with $A$ being a linear operator between appropriate vector spaces. We will consider the domain to be a non-reflexive Banach Space and the co-domain to be a space of real-valued functions on a metric space $X$. The function $g$ is characterized by a finite number of independently and identically distributed data points, which are assumed to follow some unknown probability measure $ρ$. We employ Tikhonov regularization with an arbitrary convex functional to obtain the regularized solution corresponding to the given data point. The convergence analysis is carried out with respect to the Bregman distance, and an upper bound for the error is derived in probability terms. The theoretical findings are then supported by numerical experiments.

</details>


### [124] [Energy dissipation and stability of a finite-volume scheme for two-phase flow models with dynamic capillary pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17537)
*Ansgar Jüngel,Josipa-Pina Milišić,Sara Xhahysa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于退化伪抛物交叉扩散方程组的隐式欧拉有限体积格式。该方程组描述多孔介质中具有动态毛管压力的非饱和两相流混合物的动力学行为。数值格式基于两点通量近似，保持能量结构、确保总质量守恒，并保证含水饱和度的严格正性和有界性。文中证明了离散解的存在唯一性及网格一致有界性，并通过二维数值实验展示了动态毛管压力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理具有动态毛管压力的非饱和两相流问题涉及退化伪抛物交叉扩散方程，这类方程在理论上和数值上都具有挑战性，需要设计能够保持物理性质（如能量结构、质量守恒、饱和度有界性）的数值格式。

Method: 采用隐式欧拉时间离散结合两点通量近似的有限体积空间离散方法；针对非线性组分精心选取均值函数以保证能量结构保持、总质量守恒以及含水饱和度的严格正性和有界性。

Result: 证明了离散解的存在唯一性并获得额外的网格一致有界性；二维数值实验表明该格式有效，并展示了动态毛管压力对两相流动力学的影响。

Conclusion: 该隐式欧拉有限体积格式能够稳定、保物理地求解退化伪抛物交叉扩散方程，为带动态毛管压力的多孔介质两相流问题提供了可靠的数值工具。

Abstract: An implicit Euler finite-volume scheme for degenerate pseudo-parabolic cross-diffusion equations is proposed and analyzed. The system describes the dynamics of an unsaturated two-phase flow mixture with dynamic capillary pressure in a porous medium. The numerical scheme is based on a two-point flux approximation that preserves the energy structure, ensures the conservation of total mass, and guarantees strict positivity and boundedness of the water saturation. These properties rely on carefully selected mean functions for the nonlinear components. The existence and uniqueness of a discrete solution and additional mesh-uniform bounds are proved. Numerical experiments in two space dimensions illustrate the effects of the dynamic capillary pressure.

</details>


### [125] [Mixed Finite Element Methods for a Dirac Source: Divergence-Form Splitting and L^p Error Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17575)
*Yueyao Wu,Shun Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对混合有限元法中狄拉克源问题的散度形式分裂方法，通过将量子测度从守恒律中移除，使得修正后的通量场位于L^p空间（p<2），并证明了通量误差的拟最优逼近界及阶数h^(2/p-1)的精确收敛速度，同时提出了L^p范数下的残差等价估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合有限元方法中遇到狄拉克源时，问题本质上是对偶性的失效而非正则性的失效：守恒方程在Lebesgue空间中进行测试，而狄拉克测度不位于任何Lebesgue空间的对偶空间中。现有方法通常需要依赖系数相关的奇异解或离散狄拉克函数，且源点位置受限于网格结构。

Method: 采用散度形式分裂方法：显式构造一个散度等于狄拉克测度的场，从物理通量中减去该场，将修正后的通量作为混合未知量。减法场仅依赖于源点位置而非系数。该方法无需系数依赖的奇异解或离散狄拉克，仅改变RT_0-P_0系统的载荷向量，源点可位于顶点、系数界面或单元内部任意位置。当分裂与算子不匹配时，修正通量位于L^p（p<2）而非L^2，误差分析需离开Hilbert尺度。

Result: 证明了通量的拟最优逼近界；在拟一致网格族上通量误差精确为h^(2/p-1)阶，匹配下界来自承载源点的单个单元；在源点处进行网格加密可恢复一阶复杂度N^(-1/2)；自适应计算达到该收敛率。还证明了Lebesgue尺度下的残差范数等价性，得到可计算的L^p估计器，对混合通量和重构势可靠且局部有效。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种无需系数依赖奇异解和离散狄拉克的统一处理框架，源点位置不受网格限制，并在Lebesgue尺度下建立了完整的误差分析和可计算的估计器，为带有奇异源的混合有限元方法提供了理论保证和实用策略。

Abstract: For a mixed finite element method, a Dirac source is first a failure of duality, not of regularity: the conservation equation is tested against a Lebesgue space, and a Dirac measure lies in the dual of none. We therefore remove the measure from the conservation law by a divergence-form splitting. An explicit field whose divergence is the Dirac measure is subtracted from the physical flux, and the modified flux is taken as the mixed unknown, so that only the regular part of the load remains in the conservation equation. Equivalently, and independently of any discretization, the Dirac problem is rewritten as an elliptic equation whose data are in divergence form, generated by a field of L^p. The subtracted field depends on the location of the source alone and not on the coefficient. No coefficient-dependent singular solution and no discrete delta is needed, only the load vector of the RT_0-P_0 system changes, and the source may sit anywhere relative to the mesh: at a vertex, on a coefficient interface, or inside an element. Unless the splitting is matched to the operator at the source, the modified flux lies in L^p for every p<2 but not in L^2, so the flux error analysis has to leave the Hilbert scale. We prove a quasi-best approximation bound for the flux, and with it that on a quasi-uniform family the flux error is exactly of order h^(2/p-1): the matching lower bound comes already from the single element carrying the source. Grading the mesh there restores first-order complexity, N^(-1/2) in the number of elements, and the adaptive computations attain it. The scalar variable is limited only by piecewise constant approximation of the solution, which it attains. We also prove a residual norm equivalence in the Lebesgue scale, yielding a computable L^p estimator, reliable and locally efficient for the mixed flux together with a recovered potential.

</details>


### [126] [Fast high-order solvers for the Lippmann--Schwinger equation in piecewise-smooth heterogeneous media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17615)
*Thomas G. Anderson,Juan Burbano-Gallegos,Luiz M. Faria,Carlos Pérez-Arancibia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于二维Lippmann-Schwinger方程的快速高阶Nyström求解器，结合非结构化网格上的Newton位势高阶求值与结构化网格快速预条件策略，实现高精度、准线性复杂度且迭代次数几乎与网格尺寸和波数无关的求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维时间谐波散射中可穿透、分段光滑非均匀介质的Lippmann-Schwinger方程求解面临两大挑战：非结构化网格上的高阶精度的Newton位势求值困难，以及传统快速方法在精度上的限制。需要一种既能保持几何灵活性和高阶精度，又能利用快速结构化求解器效率的方法。

Method: 方法由三部分构成：(1) 在适配不连续界面的非结构化网格上实现高阶Newton位势求值，结合快速多极子等快速算法；(2) 构建Nyström离散（曲边非结构化网格上的高阶求积节点）与底层均匀Cartesian网格快速预条件器之间的传输算子对；(3) 通过传输算子将结构化网格求解器的频率鲁棒性继承到非结构化Nyström离散上，并证明在网格尺寸与Cartesian预条件器的显式条件下预条件器的可逆性。

Result: 理论分析证明了预条件器可逆性的显式条件；数值实验表明预条件系统较未预条件系统显著减少GMRES迭代次数，迭代次数几乎与网格尺寸和波数无关，且对具有分段光滑折射率和界面跳变不连续的非均匀介质展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该预条件策略成功地将结构化网格快速求解器的频率鲁棒性与非结构化Nyström离散的高阶精度和几何灵活性相结合，为分段光滑非均匀介质散射问题提供了一种高效、鲁棒的迭代求解框架。

Abstract: This article presents a fast, high-order Nyström solver for the two-dimensional Lippmann--Schwinger equation arising from time-harmonic scattering by penetrable, piecewise-smooth heterogeneous media. Relying on high-order evaluation of the Newtonian potential on unstructured grids adapted to interfaces of discontinuity, the methodology achieves high-order accuracy using existing fast algorithms such as the fast multipole method. As an iterative method the solver exhibits rapid convergence when coupled to a preconditioning strategy that exploits a class of structured-grid solvers---fast solution methods offering quasi-linear time and memory complexity and nearly-constant iteration counts, but long limited in accuracy. The preconditioning strategy couples the Nyström discretization---given by high-order quadrature nodes over a (curved) unstructured mesh conforming to the support of the spatially varying contrast---to a uniform Cartesian grid underlying the fast preconditioner via a pair of transfer operators. The resulting preconditioner inherits the frequency-robust behavior of its Cartesian counterpart without sacrificing the geometric flexibility and high-order accuracy of the unstructured discretization. We prove that invertibility of the proposed preconditioner holds under explicit conditions on the mesh sizes and on the Cartesian preconditioner. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the preconditioned system requires significantly fewer GMRES iterations than its unpreconditioned counterpart, with iteration counts almost independent of mesh size and wavenumber, and illustrate the method's robustness for inhomogeneities with piecewise-smooth refractive indices and jump discontinuities across interfaces.

</details>


### [127] [Joint Near-Field Holotomography Reconstruction with a Phase-Guided Bregman TV Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17621)
*Jin Liu,Johannes Hagemann,Martin Burger*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于全非线性波传播模型的近场全息层析（near-field holotomography）直接变分重建框架，避免了中间相位恢复和弱物体线性化近似；并在此基础上发展了一个相位引导的Bregman TV正则化框架来改善弱吸收特征的定量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近场全息层析的逆问题本质上非线性且不适定。传统方法需要进行中间相位恢复或采用弱物体近似下的线性化，这些简化会引入误差，限制定量重建精度，特别是对弱吸收特征的重建。因此需要一种直接基于全非线性波传播模型的重建方法。

Method: 1) 在适当的Banach空间中分析正算子，建立其Fréchet可微性，并推导变分重建的显式梯度表达式；2) 开发相位引导的Bregman TV正则化框架，利用相位与吸收分量之间的结构相关性，实现多材料重建而无需对两分量施加全局固定比例。

Result: 在合成幻影和实验数据上的数值研究展示了稳定的三维重建结果，并且相比现有最先进方法，在吸收对比度的恢复方面有所改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的直接非线性变分重建框架结合相位引导Bregman TV正则化，能够避免传统方法的简化局限性，实现更稳定的三维重建和更优的吸收对比度恢复，为近场全息层析提供了一种更精确的定量重建途径。

Abstract: Near-field holotomography combines coherent diffraction imaging with tomographic acquisition to recover the three-dimensional complex refractive index of a specimen. Since the measured diffraction intensities are generated by a nonlinear object transmission and wave propagation process, the resulting inverse problem is intrinsically nonlinear and ill-posed. We study a direct variational reconstruction framework based on a fully nonlinear wave propagation model that avoids both intermediate phase retrieval and linearization under the weak-object approximation. We analyze the forward operator in appropriate Banach spaces, establish its Fréchet differentiability, and derive explicit gradient expressions for variational reconstruction. To improve quantitative reconstruction of weak absorption features, we further develop a phase-guided Bregman TV regularization framework that exploits structural correlations between phase and absorption components. This enables multi-material reconstruction without imposing a globally fixed ratio between the two components. We perform numerical studies on synthetic phantoms and experimental data. The results demonstrate stable three-dimensional reconstructions and improved recovery of the absorption contrast compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [128] [On Robust Alpha-Damping Viscous Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17664)
*Hiroaki Nishikawa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于α阻尼格式的粘性离散格式在非结构化网格上的隐式缺陷校正求解器的收敛性，发现将阻尼项在边中点而非面心处求值可显著提升迭代收敛的鲁棒性，并通过一维傅里叶分析和二维粘性流动数值实验加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式缺陷校正求解器在非结构化网格上的收敛鲁棒性不足是CFD中的一个关键难题，特别是α阻尼格式中阻尼项的求值位置对求解器稳定性的影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 采用一维傅里叶分析研究隐式求解器的稳定性条件，揭示残差中阻尼项与Jacobian构造中阻尼项的相对大小对稳定性的影响；随后在高度不规则混合单元和三角形网格上进行二维粘性流动问题的数值验证。

Result: 发现当残差中的阻尼项小于Jacobian构造所用的阻尼项时，隐式求解器趋于稳定；边中点求值方式通过有效降低残差中阻尼项的幅值来实现稳定化，并在高度不规则网格上展示了鲁棒的收敛性能。

Conclusion: 将α阻尼项的求值位置从面心改为边中点可显著提高隐式缺陷校正求解器在非结构化网格上的收敛鲁棒性，为粘性离散格式的稳定求解提供了一种简单有效的改进策略。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the convergence of an implicit defect-correction solver for a viscous discretization based on the alpha-damping scheme for unstructured grids. We show that significantly more robust iterative convergence is achieved by evaluating the damping term at the midpoint between two adjacent cell centers (edge midpoint) rather than at the face centroid. A one-dimensional Fourier analysis reveals that the implicit solver tends to be stable when the damping term in the residual is smaller than that used to construct the Jacobian. This observation suggests that the solver can be stabilized by effectively reducing the magnitude of the damping term in the residual - an effect achieved by the edge-midpoint evaluation. Robust convergence is demonstrated numerically for two-dimensional viscous-flow problems on highly irregular mixed-element and triangular grids.

</details>


### [129] [Duality-Based $\textit{A Posteriori}$ Error Identities for Subgradient Flows Based on the Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17845)
*Harbir Antil,Alex Kaltenbach,Keegan L. A. Kirk*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对由时间依赖凸积分泛函诱导的一大类次梯度流，基于Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles原理推导了对偶性后验误差恒等式，并应用于多个物理变分问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非稳态（时间依赖）设置中，传统的基于对偶性的后验误差控制框架需要被重新审视。Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles公式揭示了一个非稳态特征：原始最小值和对偶最大值均由初始数据规定，这为从联合对偶间隙恒等式分离出独立的原始和对偶间隙恒等式提供了可能，从而实现原始误差与对偶误差的独立量化。

Method: 从Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles原理出发，构建非稳态原始能量泛函并推导其Fenchel对偶公式（含强对偶及最优性系统，在一般法向被积函数假设下）。利用该Fenchel对偶框架推导次梯度流的后验误差恒等式，将联合原始-对偶间隙恒等式分解为独立的原始和对偶间隙恒等式，并以广义Bregman散度和非负时空积分量（可在空间共轭公式下进行局部化）表示。

Result: 推导出了次梯度流的原始和对偶后验误差恒等式，这些恒等式独立量化原始误差与对偶误差，并可表示为广义Bregman散度及非负时空积分量。该抽象框架成功应用于非稳态热方程、非稳态Stokes方程、非稳态Navier-Lamé方程、非稳态管道Bingham流、非稳态障碍问题及非稳态弹塑性扭转问题。

Conclusion: 通过Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles原理揭示的非稳态特征（初始数据同时规定对偶极值），使得可以建立独立的后验误差恒等式，为广泛的次梯度流问题提供了统一且可局部化的误差量化工具。

Abstract: We derive duality-based $\textit{a posteriori}$ error identities for a broad class of subgradient flows induced by time-dependent convex integral functionals. Starting from the Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles principle, we identify an unsteady primal energy functional and derive its Fenchel dual formulation, including strong duality and the corresponding optimality system under general normal-integrand assumptions. This Fenchel duality framework is used to derive $\textit{a posteriori}$ error identities for subgradient flows. In doing so, we depart from the usual duality-based $\textit{a posteriori}$ error control framework in the unsteady setting, since the Brézis-Ekeland-Nayroles formulation reveals the following unsteady feature: the minimal primal value and the maximal dual value are both prescribed by the initial datum. This allows us to pass from a combined primal-dual gap identity to separate primal and dual gap identities. These identities quantify the primal and dual errors independently and admit representations in terms of generalized Bregman divergences and, under a spatial convex conjugation formula, as non-negative time-space integral quantities suitable for localization. The abstract framework is applied to a number of variational problems of physical interest, including the unsteady heat equation, the unsteady Stokes equations, the unsteady Navier-Lamé equations, the unsteady Bingham flow through a pipe, the unsteady obstacle problem, and the unsteady elasto-plastic torsion problem.

</details>


### [130] [Efficient computation of eddy-currents for nonlinear magnetic field problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17860)
*Herbert Egger,Nepomuk Krenn,Andreas Schafelner*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文回顾了电气设备中非叠片导电部件涡流损耗的各种近似估算方法，指出涡流反应场是需全局考虑的重要贡献，并提出可从二维静磁仿真出发求解一个线性化时周期问题来近似重构该反应场，以中等额外计算成本实现更优的损耗预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电气设备中非叠片导电部件的涡流损耗估算通常需要昂贵的三维仿真，在早期设计阶段计算成本过高，因此需要开发近似方法来降低计算开销，同时保持足够的预测精度。

Method: 本文首先回顾了几种涡流损耗近似方法并讨论其建模假设，指出涡流反应场是需要全局考虑的显著贡献；提出通过二维静磁仿真求解一个线性化时周期问题来近似重构反应场，并讨论了该后处理问题的不同求解策略。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的方法相比标准后处理方法在损耗预测精度上有所提升，且额外计算成本适中。

Conclusion: 基于二维静态磁仿真加线性化时周期后处理的策略，能够在适度的额外计算开销下有效改善非叠片导电部件涡流损耗的预测精度，为早期设计阶段的快速评估提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Estimation of eddy-current losses in conducting non-laminated components of electrical devices requires expensive three-dimensional simulations. Various approximations are therefore used in practice to reduce the computational cost in the early design phase. We review some approaches and discuss their modelling assumptions and resulting approximations. In particular, we identify eddy-current reaction fields as a significant contribution that should be accounted for globally. These reaction fields can be approximately reconstructed from two-dimensional magnetostatic simulations by solving a single linearized time-periodic problem. We further discuss different strategies for solving this post-processing problem. Numerical results demonstrate improved loss prediction compared to standard post-processing at moderate additional cost.

</details>


### [131] [Area-Preserving Parameterization: Variational Principle, Gradient Flow, and Discrete Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17873)
*Shu-Yung Liu,Kento Sakai,Mei-Heng Yueh*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文通过拉伸能量研究保面积参数化问题，证明了拉伸能量可由面积比的方差刻画且其临界点即保面积映射，据此提出L²梯度流（authalic flow）及其离散化算法，并证明了离散拉伸能量的一阶一致性和离散全局极小值的一阶L²面积畸变界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保面积参数化在需要保持相对表面面积的应用（如纹理映射、形状分析等）中至关重要，但已有方法缺乏严格的变分理论基础，也缺少与光滑理论的连接。

Method: 将保面积参数化问题建模为拉伸能量的变分最小化问题；证明拉伸能量由面积比方差刻画，其临界点即保面积映射；由此导出L²梯度流（authalic flow）；开发基于离散拉伸能量的单纯复形对应算法，适用于多种拓扑类型的开、闭曲面；并证明离散拉伸能量的一阶一致性及离散全局极小值的L²面积畸变界。

Result: 在基准网格上的数值实验中，所有测试均产生无折叠的映射，面积保持效果优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为保面积参数化提供了严格的变分理论基础，建立了离散与光滑理论之间的连接，并给出了实用且鲁棒的数值算法。

Abstract: Area-preserving parameterizations are used in applications where relative surface areas must be preserved. We study this problem through the stretch energy. For orientation-preserving diffeomorphisms between compact Riemannian 2-manifolds of equal total area, we show that the stretch energy is characterized by the variance of the area ratio and that its critical points are area-preserving. This variational characterization leads naturally to an $L^2$-gradient flow, which we call the authalic flow. We then develop its simplicial counterpart based on the discrete stretch energy and obtain computational methods for open and closed surfaces of several topological types. To connect the discrete formulation with the smooth theory, we prove the first-order consistency of the stretch energy with respect to mesh refinement and establish a first-order $L^2$ area-distortion bound for discrete global minimizers under the stated geometric approximation assumptions. Numerical experiments on benchmark meshes produce fold-free maps in all reported tests and show competitive area preservation compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [132] [Asymptotic dispersion correction for the isotropic elastic Helmholtz equation discretized with a MAC scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17909)
*Pierre-Henri Cocquet,Antoine Tonnoir,Rachel Yovel*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对各向同性弹性Helmholtz方程的MAC离散格式，通过分析其离散色散关系，推导出一种渐近最优的色散修正方法，在二维和三维空间中均可减小数值色散和污染效应，同时保持原离散结构，并提升多网格求解器的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性介质中时间谐波波动问题的数值模拟在地球物理和无损检测等领域至关重要，但高频下弹性Helmholtz方程的精确离散因数值色散和污染效应而极具挑战性，亟需有效的色散修正方法。

Method: 作者对MAC格式离散的弹性Helmholtz方程进行了离散色散关系分析，确定二维和三维空间中色散误差的领头阶项；基于此推导出在网格尺寸趋于零时渐近最优的色散修正，同时保持原离散格式结构；并揭示了色散关系因式分解与梯度-散度算子符号结构之间的代数联系。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的修正显著降低了相对误差，证实了方法的有效性；同时提供了数值证据表明修正后的离散格式改善了多网格求解器的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的渐近色散修正方法能在保持MAC离散格式结构的同時有效提升离散与连续波动性质的一致性，并改善多网格求解收敛性能，为弹性Helmholtz方程的高频数值模拟提供了一种实用且有效的方案。

Abstract: The numerical simulation of time-harmonic wave propagation in elastic media plays an important role in applications such as geophysics and non-destructive testing. Accurate discretization of the elastic Helmholtz equation at high frequencies is challenging due to numerical dispersion and pollution effects. In this work, we develop an asymptotic dispersion correction for a Marker-And-Cell (MAC) discretization of the isotropic elastic Helmholtz equation. We characterize the discrete dispersion relation of the scheme and determine the leading-order term in the dispersion error in both two and three spatial dimensions. Based on this analysis, we derive a correction that is asymptotically optimal in the limit of vanishing mesh size. The proposed approach improves the agreement between the discrete and continuous wave propagation properties while preserving the structure of the underlying discretization. We also establish a connection between the factorization of the dispersion relation and the structure of the grad-div operator symbol, providing additional insight into the algebraic structure of the elastic problem. Numerical experiments finally demonstrate a substantial reduction of relative errors and confirm the effectiveness of the proposed correction. We further provide numerical evidence that the corrected discretization improves the convergence behavior of multigrid solvers.

</details>


### [133] [From complex-step differentiation to a general reconstruction framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17924)
*Rafael Abreu,Chahana Nagesh*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文基于Cauchy-Riemann方程对复步进法进行了全新的理论重构，表明该数值微分方法本质上源于全纯函数的调和结构。通过将边界重构问题推广到上半平面，作者将复步进法与Poisson、Hilbert、Cauchy核以及Stieltjes变换及其逆变换联系起来，并进一步扩展到谱理论中，建立了一个连接经典数值微分与解析连续化的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的复步进法从Taylor展开推导而来，其理论依据较为局域且缺乏结构性理解。作者希望揭示该方法的更深层数学本质，将数值微分技术与调和分析、边界重构问题以及谱理论统一起来，提供一个更具普适性的理论框架，同时为高效的数值实现（如FFT）奠定基础。

Method: 作者首先利用Cauchy-Riemann方程将复步进法重新表述为上半平面上的边界重构问题，即给定边界值（实轴上的函数）去重构上半平面内的调和函数。这一构造自然引出Poisson核（调和重构）、Hilbert核（共轭调和函数）和Cauchy核（全纯重构）作为基本重构算子。随后将边界对象从普通函数推广到有限测度，得到经典的Stieltjes变换及其反演公式。最后展示了相同的重构原理在谱理论中的应用：解析矩阵元的预解式对应于谱测度的Stieltjes变换，从而将复步进法、Stieltjes反演、预解方法及半经典分析统一起来，并提出了基于FFT的数值实现方案。

Result: 论文从理论上证明了经典的复步进近似自然地由全纯函数的调和结构导出，建立了复步进法与Poisson、Hilbert和Cauchy核之间的等价关系；将框架推广至测度情形后得到了Stieltjes变换及其反演公式；在谱理论中揭示了标量矩阵元预解式与谱测度Stieltjes变换之间的直接对应。此外，提出了基于FFT的高效数值实现方法用于所需的解析延拓计算。

Conclusion: 该工作为复步进法提供了一个统一的调和分析理论基础，将其与边界重构、Stieltjes反演、谱理论和半经典分析建立了实质性联系。这不仅深化了对经典数值方法的理论理解，也为谱信息的高效数值恢复提供了新的途径，展示了复微扰技术在多个数学和物理分支中的统一本质。

Abstract: The complex-step method is traditionally derived from the Taylor expansion of an analytic function and it is widely used as a numerical technique for derivative approximation. We present an alternative formulation based on the Cauchy--Riemann equations. We show that the classical complex-step approximation arises naturally from the harmonic structure of holomorphic functions and formulate the corresponding boundary reconstruction problem in the upper half-plane. This construction naturally leads to the Poisson, Hilbert, and Cauchy kernels as the elementary reconstruction operators for harmonic and holomorphic functions. Extending the boundary reconstruction from ordinary functions to finite measures on obtains the classical Stieltjes transform and its inversion formula. We further show that the same reconstruction principle naturally extends to spectral theory, where scalar matrix elements of the resolvent are Stieltjes transforms of the associated spectral measures. This provides a direct connection between the complex-step method, Stieltjes inversion, resolvent methods, and semiclassical analysis, where the same complex perturbation underlies the recovery of spectral information. Finally, we discuss an FFT-based implementation for the numerical evaluation of the required analytic continuation.

</details>


### [134] [Physics-Informed Learning of Probabilistic Gegenbauer Reconstruction for Transport-Dominated Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18001)
*Lei Yan,Yan Jiang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个物理信息机器学习框架，用于预测候选Gegenbauer参数对的概率分布，实现对传输主导问题的概率加权重建，从而减少数值振荡并提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传输主导问题对数据驱动方法具有挑战性，现有方法在激波或陡梯度附近常出现严重数值振荡。Gegenbauer重建虽有潜力，但其效果高度依赖参数选择（权重参数λ和截断阶数m），而数据驱动场景中不同的控制方程、训练数据和模型架构使系统化参数选择尤为困难。

Method: 提出物理信息机器学习框架，预测候选Gegenbauer参数对的概率分布，实现考虑参数不确定性的概率加权重建。采用两阶段策略：先预训练通用预测器，再针对目标问题微调，以平衡精度与计算成本。框架分别用于POD-Galerkin（降阶模型）和DeepONet（神经算子模型）进行评估。

Result: 在一维和二维传输主导问题上的数值实验表明，该框架能学习有效的空间自适应参数分布。与传统重建策略相比，数值误差降低1-2个数量级，且相比针对特定问题的模型重训练，实现了更优的精度-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架通过概率化处理Gegenbauer参数不确定性，有效缓解了数据驱动方法在传输主导问题中的数值振荡，同时以两阶段策略兼顾了精度与计算效率，为复杂传输问题的数据驱动建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Transport-dominated problems remain challenging for data-driven methods, which often exhibit severe numerical oscillations near shocks or steep gradients due to globally supported basis functions or overly smooth hypothesis spaces. Gegenbauer reconstruction has shown promise in mitigating such oscillations, but its effectiveness critically depends on the reconstruction parameters, particularly the weight parameter $λ$ and truncation order $m$. For data-driven models, variations in governing problems, training data, and model architectures make systematic parameter selection particularly challenging.
  To address this issue, we propose a physics-informed machine-learning framework that predicts probability distributions over candidate Gegenbauer parameter pairs, enabling probabilistically weighted reconstruction while accounting for parameter uncertainty. A two-stage strategy is adopted, in which a general predictor is first pre-trained and then fine-tuned for target problems to balance accuracy and computational cost. The framework is evaluated for reduced-order and neural operator models, represented by POD-Galerkin and DeepONet, respectively. Numerical experiments on one- and two-dimensional transport-dominated problems show that the framework learns effective spatially adaptive parameter distributions. Compared with conventional reconstruction strategies, it reduces numerical errors by up to one to two orders of magnitude and achieves a more favorable accuracy--cost trade-off than problem-specific model retraining.

</details>


### [135] [Ordered Diffusion Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18019)
*Jack H. Soulsby,Andreas C. S. Jørgensen,Atiyo Ghosh,Vahid Shahrezaei*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出有序扩散核（Ordered Diffusion Kernels, ODKs），一种新型局部核，可逼近任意Itô随机微分方程（SDE）的无穷小生成元。ODKs通过推断数据排序而非估计势函数，解耦平流与扩散项，适用于动力学先验信息缺乏的数据采样系统，并支持多种扩展（各向异性扩散、局部排序函数、多种序函数、自适应带宽等），在合成数据上验证了算子、速度场、曲率及空间依赖漂移扩散系数的准确恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的扩散核Laplacian仅逼近Laplace-Beltrami算子；调整归一化可引入依赖于采样密度的平流项；近期TMDmap虽将其推广至任意测度，但平流与扩散耦合。更一般的局部核可学习任意二阶椭圆算子，但需要已知速度场，使得推断问题可能不适定。因此需要一种不耦合平流与扩散、且不需要预先知道速度场的局部核构造方法。

Method: 首先将势函数估计这一不适定问题松弛为更易处理的数据排序推断问题，通过排序函数表示；证明ODK的Laplacian收敛到具有状态依赖各向同性扩散的梯度流SDE的无穷小生成元，且平流与扩散解耦。扩展包括：通过局部排序函数处理任意漂移、通过Strang分裂处理各向异性扩散、多排序函数及自适应带宽；并提出两种利用ODK结构的损失函数以求解非参数推断问题。

Result: 在确定性系统和随机系统的合成数据上验证了框架的有效性，准确恢复了算子、速度场、外在曲率以及空间依赖的漂移和扩散系数。

Conclusion: ODKs为从缺乏动力学先验的数据中推断SDE生成元提供了新的框架，解决了经典扩散核和现有方法中平流-扩散耦合及势估计不适定等核心问题，并通过多种扩展大幅增强了其适用性。

Abstract: We introduce Ordered Diffusion Kernels (ODKs), a novel class of local kernels that can approximate the infinitesimal generator of an arbitrary Itô Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE). ODKs are designed to be applied to data sampled from dynamical systems where little dynamical information is available a priori. The Laplacian of classical diffusion kernels approximates the Laplace-Beltrami operator on the underlying manifold; adjusting the normalisation introduces an advection term that depends on the sampling density; recently, TMDmap generalised this normalisation to target an arbitrary measure, but at the cost of coupling advection to diffusion. More general local kernels can learn arbitrary second-order elliptic operators but are formulated in terms of known velocity fields --- making the first step of any analysis a potentially ill-posed inference problem. To formulate ODK, we first relax the problem of potential estimation to the more tractable task of inferring an ordering of the data, which we represent through an ordering function. We prove ODK's Laplacian converges to the infinitesimal generator of a gradient-flow SDE with state-dependent isotropic diffusion, without coupling advection and diffusion. We provide various extensions of ODK to: arbitrary drifts via local ordering functions; anisotropic diffusions via a Strang splitting scheme; multiple ordering functions; and self-tuning bandwidths. In addition, we introduce two loss functions which exploit the structure of ODKs to solve a non-parametric inference problem. We validate this framework on synthetic data from deterministic and stochastic systems, demonstrating accurate recovery of operators, velocity fields, extrinsic curvature, and spatially dependent drift and diffusion coefficients.

</details>
